    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [u1].[Id], [u1].[DateCreation], [u1].[DateSuppression], [u1].[DerniereConnexion], [u1].[Email], [u1].[EstSupprime], [u1].[MotDePasseHash], [u1].[Nom], [u1].[OtpExpiration], [u1].[OtpSecret], [u1].[Prenom], [u1].[Role], [u1].[Statut], [u1].[Telephone], [u1].[TelephoneVerifie], [u1].[TentativesConnexionEchouees], [u1].[VerrouilleJusquA]

    WHERE [c].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [s0] ON [s].[Id] = [s0].[BienId]
